|1+i\epsilon f(s)|\leq 1
for
\epsilon\ll 1
implies that
\text{Im}f(s)\geq 0
. In our case the small parameter
\epsilon
is
\frac{1}{\ell^{3}}
.

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\left(\alpha^{(1)}_{abc}\text{Im}(\rho^{a}(s)\chi^{2}(s))+\alpha^{(2)}_{abc}\text{Im}(\rho^{a}(s))\right)b\sqrt{s}\,\,\rho^{c}(-s)\geq 0,}

(371)

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\left(\alpha^{(1)}_{abc}\text{Im}(\rho^{a}(s)\chi^{2}(s))-\alpha^{(2)}_{abc}\text{Im}(\rho^{a}(s))\right)b\sqrt{s}\,\,\rho^{c}(-s)\geq 0,}

(372)

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(1)}_{cba}\text{Im}(\rho^{a}(s))\,b\sqrt{s}\,\,\rho^{c}(-s)\chi^{2}(-s)\geq 0,}

(373)

valid for
s\geq 0
.

H.2
Large energy

We begin by considering the
s\rightarrow\infty
expansion of a monomial term in the ansatz:

\displaystyle\rho^{a}(s)\rho^{b}(t)\rho^{c}(u)\approx(-1)^{a+b+c}\Bigg[1

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{\sqrt{s}}\left(ia+\frac{\sqrt{2}b}{\sqrt{1-z}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}c}{\sqrt{1+z}}\right)+i\frac{4\sqrt{2}a}{s}\left(\frac{b}{\sqrt{1-z}}+\frac{c}{\sqrt{1+z}}\right)

(374)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{s}\left(-2a^{2}+\frac{8bc}{\sqrt{1-z}\sqrt{1+z}}+\frac{4b^{2}}{1-z}+\frac{4c^{2}}{1+z}\right)+\ldots

In addition to the large energy expansion of the
\rho
series, we also need the large energy expansion of
\chi^{2}(s)
,
\chi^{2}(u)
and
\chi(t)\chi(u)
:

\displaystyle\chi^{2}(s)

\displaystyle\approx 9-\frac{48i}{\sqrt{s}}+\ldots

(375)

\displaystyle\chi^{2}(u)

\displaystyle\approx 9-\frac{48i}{\sqrt{s}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+z}}+\ldots

\displaystyle\chi(s)\chi(t)\chi(u)

\displaystyle\approx 27-\frac{72i}{\sqrt{s}}\left(1+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{1+z}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{1-z}}\right)+\ldots

This leads to the following expansions of the ansatze at large energy:

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}

\displaystyle\approx\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(1)}_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}\Bigg[9-\frac{6}{\sqrt{s}}\left((8+3a)i+\frac{3\sqrt{2}b}{\sqrt{1-z}}+\frac{3\sqrt{2}c}{\sqrt{1+z}}\right)+\ldots\Bigg],

(376)

\displaystyle\Phi_{2}

\displaystyle\approx\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(2)}_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}\Bigg[1-\frac{2}{\sqrt{s}}\left(ia+\frac{\sqrt{2}b}{\sqrt{1-z}}+\frac{\sqrt{2}c}{\sqrt{1+z}}\right)+\ldots\Bigg],

\displaystyle\Phi_{3}

\displaystyle\approx\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(1)}_{cba}(-1)^{a+b+c}\Bigg[9-\frac{6}{\sqrt{s}}\left(3ia+\frac{3\sqrt{2}b}{\sqrt{1-z}}+\frac{(8+3c)\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{1+z}}\right)+\ldots\Bigg],

\displaystyle\Phi_{5}

\displaystyle\approx\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(5)}_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}\Bigg[27-\frac{18}{\sqrt{s}}\left((4+3a)i+\frac{(4+3b)\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{1-z}}+\frac{(4+3c)\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{1+z}}\right)+\ldots\Bigg]~.

We now work order by order in
\frac{1}{s}
. We begin with the leading order which is

O(1):

The unitarity condition on spin
0
partial waves
|1+i(\Phi^{0}_{1}\pm\Phi^{0}_{2})|\leq 1
implies that

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(1)}_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}=0}~,

(377)

and

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(2)}_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}=0}~.

(378)

Similarly, the unitarity condition on odd spin
\ell
partial waves
|1+2i(\Phi^{\ell}_{3})|\leq 1
implies that

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(1)}_{cba}(-1)^{a+b+c}=0}~,

(379)

which turns out to be equivalent to the condition (
377
).
Finally the unitarity condition (
139
) simplifies at leading order due to the results we just derived above, and leads to

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(5)}_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}=0}~.

(380)

Effectively, the constraints above say that constant terms in the amplitudes should go to 0 as
s\rightarrow\infty
. We now consider the next order which is

O(
s^{-1/2}
):

From the unitarity condition on spin 0 partial waves
|1+i(\Phi^{0}_{1}\pm\Phi^{0}_{2})|\leq 1
, we get the two conditions:

\boxed{\sum_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}\left((24+9a)\,\alpha_{abc}^{(1)}+a\,\alpha_{abc}^{(2)}\right)\leq 0}~,

(381)

and

\boxed{\sum_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}\left((24+9a)\,\alpha_{abc}^{(1)}-a\,\alpha_{abc}^{(2)}\right)\leq 0}~.

(382)

Consider now the unitarity condition (
139
) for non-zero even spin
\ell
partial waves. The imaginary part of the
\Phi^{\ell}_{1}
and
\Phi^{\ell}_{2}
is 0 and this immediately implies that the imaginary part of
\Phi_{5}
must vanish at this order:

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(5)}_{abc}(-1)^{a+b+c}a=0}~.

(383)

In addition, it implies that
|1+2\phi^{\ell}_{3}|\leq 1
.
50
50

50

More precisely, it implies it only for even
\ell
, but we also have the same condition
|1+2\phi^{\ell}_{3}|\leq 1
separately for odd
\ell
from (
137
).
Since
\int dz\,d^{\ell}_{22}(z)>0
for even
\ell
and
\int dz\,d^{\ell}_{22}(z)<0
for odd
\ell
, we get

\boxed{\sum_{abc}\alpha^{(1)}_{cba}(-1)^{a+b+c}a=0}~.

(384)
